# TODO

## Notes

Ensure test coverage because ValueErrors are due to user input, therefore every ValueError
should be raise-able.  

### Playwright test cases:  
Create a new Forecast.  
Edit transactions.  
Run the forecast.  
Verify the report loads.  
Create a ForecastSet.  
Compare multiple scenarios.  
Export a report.  
Confirm no JavaScript errors occurred.  

### My Suggested First UI Refactor
I would start with the New Forecast view only:  
Convert it into a left-to-right or top-to-bottom setup flow.  
Keep the existing PHP handlers for now.  
Replace repeated form sections with editable tables.  
Add a review panel before stage/run.  

### Known Issues
A refactor of the loan allocaiton algorthm now overallocates by a few pennies in certain cases where marginal interest for multiple loans are very close together

## Human Tasks
- Confirm that AccountMilestones are able to target sub accounts
- Decide if i want doctests in comments
- ValueError instead of TypeError ?
- i think min and max balance fields should belong to the billing state classes
- review constructor docstring and remove Returns section
- when docstrings are done, review once more to decide what belong in module / class / __init__
- project global replace of budget_Set to line_item_set

## LLM Tasks
 - Update docstrings
 - make it so Infinity not allowed on account min balance but it IS allowed on account max balance.
 - #Codex-write-doctstring-OK
 - "TODO DEFER change from AssertionError to ValueError with error message including the illegal values"
 - use Decimal instead of float everywhere
 - in _propagate_credit_txn_curr_only I see account name manipulation via splitting on ':' and that is a sign to me
 that old behavior has not been fully removed
 - Add Policies to report
 - Not all plots have below or above text, but changing this will require a customization of the methods used for rendering.
 - x axis ticks on the first of each year showing the year
 - TODO DEFER CODEX-OK

## Roadmap
 - Benchmarks
 - Step Size
 - Investment case
